As far as it spreads, filled with wrath,
So many does it bring to ruin.
Likewise, its withdrawal too:
If the yogin so wishes,
To all of them without exception
He should give back their life again.

Here their heart-mantra is this:
hūṃ hūṃ nāśaya vajra hūṃ vināśaya sarva sūkṣma vajra pratyanāśaya śīghrāṃ hūṃ

Whoever greatly shows himself
As joined with a being's enemy,
In the manner of spreading loving-kindness
Destroy him with vajra emanation.
Through the yoga of vajra emanation,
Whoever is compassionate,
Through that yoga of compassion,
Surely destroys all the vicious.

Whether dharma or non-dharma,
By its very nature it is luminous.
When one has meditated in this way,
One destroys beings with hūṃ.
Whatever beings are hard to tame,
Not vessels for the awakening of the buddhas,
Then, in order to purify them properly,
One should destroy them with huṃ.

Here their heart-mantras are these:
bhaira vajre krodha huṃ phaṭ huṃ viśuddha vajra krodha huṃ phaṭ oṃ viśodhana vajra krodha huṃ phaṭ

Having drawn the shape of a vajra,
If one imagines, even in the mind,
That it falls in the middle of someone's house,
That person's family will be destroyed.
Likewise, by the subtle rite,
Having meditated on a vajra at the heart,
If the form of a great bodhisattva
Falls there, the family will be destroyed.
If one meditates on the great form of Vajrapāṇi,
Then wherever it falls, that person's
Whole world of appearance and existence, together with his queens,
Will be destroyed by all manner of ills.
Having meditated on the form of the Buddha,
Endowed with the supreme of all aspects,
Whatever appearance it falls upon,
Its whole world will surely be destroyed.

Here their heart-mantras are these:
hūṃ vajra pravata hūṃ bodhisatva pravata hūṃ vajradhara pravata huṃ buddha pravata

By applying the subtle vajra well,
Having meditated on oneself
In the form of the moon,
Wherever it falls, one falls there too.
Having meditated on oneself
As a moon vajra,
Wherever one falls in fierce wrath,
That person's family falls at once.
Having meditated on oneself
As Vajrapāṇi,
Wherever one falls, that land
Will be destroyed before long.
Having meditated on oneself
As the very form of the Buddha,
Wherever one falls, its whole world
Of appearance and existence will be destroyed before long.

Here their heart-mantras are these:
bodhāgra pratuya hūṃ svastva vajra pravataya hūṃ vajrasatva pravata huṃ buddha pravataya hūṃ

Next, one should learn the wisdom of the secret Dharma mudrās of the vajra family.

Absorbed in Vajracaṇḍa,
Encircling with one's own body,
Embracing from Vajra Hūṃ,
Whoever's name one speaks will die.
Absorbed in the subtle vajra,
Subtly from the nose, without a moment's pause,
By means of the breath and hūṃ,
Even the three worlds will fall.
Applying the method of the subtle vajra,
Whoever one looks at in wrath
With the vajra gaze will become blind,
Or else will die.
Having entered the womb,
If with the mind one draws out
The heart of anyone at all,
That one comes under one's own power or that of another.

Here he spoke their heart-mantras: hūṃ vajra valita krodha māraya hūṃ phaṭ oṃ sūkṣma vajra śvasa viśra patāya hūṃ phaṭ oṃ vajra dṛṣṭi viśa viśa vināśaya hūṃ phaṭ hūṃ hṛdaya kāna krodha praviśa kayi hṛdaya cchinda bhinda kata kata hūṃ phaṭ

Next, one should learn the wisdom of the Dharma mudrās of the vajra family. Here, first of all, the binding of the great mudrā is explained.

Through union with vajra wisdom,
With light massed in a blazing garland,
If one meditates on oneself as Vajracaṇḍa,
One will succeed at once.

Next, one should learn the wisdom of the Dharma samaya mudrās of the vajra family.

From the wisdom of samādhi and samaya,
Endowed with two syllables hūṃ,
Set in their respective places,
It is the supreme giver of all accomplishments.

Next, one should learn the wisdom of the Dharma mudrās of the Dharma samaya of the vajra family.
phaṭ śaṭ maṭ saṭ raṭ taṭ hṛṭ haṭ praṭ traṭ daṭ bhaṭ kṛṭ riṭ khaṭ vaṭ
This is the concise explanation of the Dharma mudrās.

Next, one should learn the wisdom of the karma mudrās of the Dharma samaya of the vajra family.

Making two Dharma fists,
Join them at their proper places.
In brief, the karma mudrā
Brings success in accordance with the rite.

Here is completed the explanation of the extensive rite of the wisdom samaya maṇḍala of the vajra family, from the Great King of Ritual Manuals of the Great Vajra Samaya of All Tathāgatas.

Then the Blessed One entered the samādhi called Empowerment of the Arising of the Vajra Karma Samaya of All Tathāgatas and spoke this supreme vidyā of his own: oṃ sarva tathāgata karmeśvara hūṃ

Then again the bodhisattva, the great being Vajrapāṇi, spoke this supreme vidyā of his own: oṃ sarva tathāgata dharma dhātu spharaṇa mahā būjā karma vidhi vistāra samaye trailokya vijaya kira sarva duṣṭaṃ samaya vajriṇi hūṃ

Then again the bodhisattva, the great being Vajragarbha, spoke this supreme vidyā of his own:
oṃ sarva tathāgata kāya śadu samaya spharaṇa mahā būjā karma vidhi stāraya samaye hūṃ

Then again the great bodhisattva Vajranetra spoke this supreme vidyā of his own:
oṃ sarva tathāgata dharma dhātu spharaṇa mahā būjā karma vidhi vistāra samaye hūṃ

Then again the great bodhisattva Vajraviśva spoke this supreme vidyā of his own:
oṃ sarva tathāgata sarva avaloka dhātu ma vi mahā pujā karma vidhi vistāra samaye hūṃ phaḍ

Then again the great bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi generated from his own heart the goddesses of the extensive rite of offering of his own family. Throughout all world systems they performed the buddha miracles and magical displays of all the tathāgatas, such as the perfect applications that serve the purposes of inviting all the tathāgatas, bringing them under control, pleasing them, and accomplishing all rites. Then they returned and took their places, abiding on moon disks, in the manner of the great Vajradhātu maṇḍala of the Blessed One, Vairocana.

Then the great bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi spoke this karma maṇḍala of the extensive rite of the vajra karma samaya.

Next, what is to be fully explained
Is the sublime karma maṇḍala.
Because it resembles the Vajradhātu,
It is called the Karma Vajra.

In the manner of the great maṇḍala,
Mark out the whole maṇḍala with cords.
In the place at the center of the maṇḍala
One should set the image of the Buddha.
In all directions around the Buddha
One should set the supreme samayas.
Having crossed over with vajra force,
In the four maṇḍalas as well
The four, the Vajra Lord and the rest,
Should be set in their proper order.
In all directions around them
The great offerings are set.
Then, for this karma maṇḍala,
The vajra karma mudrās are explained:

oṃ vajrasatva siddhi jñāna karma samaye hūṃ ja
oṃ vajra ākarṣaṇa karma jñāna samaye hūṃ ja
oṃ vajra rati rāga karma jñāna samaye hūṃ ja
oṃ vajra sādhu karma jñān samaye hūṃ ja
oṃ vajra bhṛkuṭī vaśiṃ kuru hūṃ
oṃ vajra sūrya maṇḍale vaśiṃ kuru hūṃ
oṃ vajra dhvajagra keyūre vaśiṃ kuru hūṃ
oṃ vajra data hāse vaśiṃ kuru hūṃ
oṃ vajra padma rāge rāgaya hūṃ
oṃ vajra tīkṣṇa rāge rāgaya hūṃ
oṃ vajra maṇḍala rāge rāgaya hūṃ
oṃ vajra vāka rāge rāgaya hūṃ
oṃ vajra karma samaya caya hūṃ
oṃ vajra kavaca vaṃdha rakṣa rakṣa hūṃ
oṃ yakṣiṇi māraya vajra daṃṣṭaya bhinda bhindaya mugasya hūṃ phaṭ
oṃ vajra karma muṣṭi siddhi hūṃ phaṭ

Then, in this karma maṇḍala, in accordance with the extensive rite, one generates the wisdoms of the vajra family. Here, first of all, one should learn the wisdom of the mudrās of pacifying and the rest.

Kindling well a fire of sweet kindling sticks,
In equipoise, absorbed in Vajracaṇḍa,
By burning sesame one burns away one's sins.
With those very kindling sticks
Having kindled the fire well,
If one constantly burns rice,
The household will without doubt prosper.
With sweet kindling sticks as well
The wise one kindles the fire well;
If one burns sprigs of dūrvā grass with butter,
One's life will increase.
With those very kindling sticks
Having kindled the fire well,
If one burns sprigs of kuśa grass with sesame oil,
One will surely be protected.

Here their heart-mantras are these:
oṃ sarva pāpaṃ dahana vajrāya svāhā oṃ vajra buṣṭaye svāhā oṃ vajra āyuṣe svāhā oṃ vajra apratihata vajrāya svāhā
teva ruva miṃ dhidhulya gani samahri sajuhe tarigataya laṃkuto mati pūtapataye ta

First, with pungent kindling sticks,
Through the samādhi of Vajracaṇḍa,
Having kindled the fire, with thorny wild rose
One will summon.
With those very kindling sticks,
If one kindles the fire filled with wrath
And performs the fire offering with many red flowers, or with fruits,
People will be transfixed.
The yogin, in wrath,
Kindles the fire with those sticks;
If he burns iron filings,
It becomes the very vajra binding.
With those very kindling sticks,
In equipoise, blow upon the fire,
And burn bitter fruits as if in wrath:
In an instant there will be death.

Here their heart-mantras are these:
hūṃ vajra ākarṣā karṣaya svāhā hūṃ vajra rāgāya svāhā hūṃ vajra bandhāya svāhā hūṃ vajra māraṇāya svāhā

The wise one, with sour kindling sticks,
Having kindled the fire in wrath,
If he burns sour fruits and leaves,
That is the supreme bringing under control.
With those very kindling sticks,
In equipoise, kindling the fire,
If one burns desired fruits in wrath,
One produces forms just as one wishes.
With such kindling sticks
Having kindled the fire well,
If one wishes to make the fire offering with stalks of the invisibility flower,
One becomes invisible.
With those very kindling sticks,
In equipoise, kindling the fire,
If one makes the fire offering with flowers of the sky creeper,
One travels through the sky.

Here their heart-mantras are these:
oṃ vajra vaśaṃ karāya svāhā oṃ vajra karma rūpa vajrāya svāhā oṃ adyaśya vajrāya svāhā oṃ vajra kha varcariṇi svāhā

In equipoise as the wrathful one, with kindling sticks
That are very bitter, kindle the fire;
If one burns vajri flowers,
The vajra beings will do as commanded.
With those very kindling sticks,
Filled with wrath, kindle the fire;
Whatever hero's garland one burns,
He too will obey one's commands.
In equipoise as the very wrathful one,
Kindle the fire with those sticks,
And burn the garland of Vajrapāṇi:
He too will act as commanded.
With those very kindling sticks,
In fierce wrath, kindle the fire;
By burning Dharma robes, even the buddhas
In an instant will act as commanded.

Here their heart-mantras are these: hūṃ vajra vaśaṃ karāya svāhā hūṃ sori vaśaṃ kara vajrāya svāhā hūṃ vajra pāni vaśaṃ karāya svahā huṃ buddha vaśaṃ karāya vajrāya svāhā

Next, one should learn the wisdom of the secret karma mudrās.

Dwelling together with a lovely woman,
Placing eye ointment in her secret opening,
And having stimulated it,
When one anoints one's eyes with that, she comes under one's control.
Placing realgar in the opening,
And covering it with the vajra binding,
Through the four kinds of signs
There will be the four kinds of accomplishment.
Placing gorocana in the opening,
If one presses it with the secret fist,
And what was placed there blazes,
One becomes like a vajra holder.
Placing saffron in the opening,
With the sattva vajra in the opening,
If, being pressed, it blazes,
One becomes like a vajra holder.

Here their heart-mantras are these: oṃ guhya rati vajra vaśaṃ kāraṃ siddhya hūṃ oṃ vajra guhya siddhya hūṃ oṃ guhya vajra siddha hūṃ oṃ vajradhara hya siddhya hūṃ

Next, one should learn the wisdom of the great karma mudrās of the vajra family.

In the manner of the vajra intention,
The great mudrā, in brief,
Is to be in equipoise as Vajracaṇḍa
In the manner of the vajra fist.
With hūṃ, in the manner of the finger,
So too is the supreme samaya here;
So too is the Dharma mudrā here:
With oṃ, the first, by the letter a.
The karma mudrā, in brief:
Making the karma mudrā twofold,
It is pure and accomplishes everything,
In the manner of vajra karma.

Here is completed the extensive rite of the karma maṇḍala of the vajra family, from the Great King of Ritual Manuals of the Vajra Samaya of All Tathāgatas.

Then again the Blessed One entered the samādhi called Empowerment of the Vajra Samaya Mudrā of All Tathāgatas and spoke this supreme vidyā of his own: oṃ sarva tathāgata vajra samaye hūṃ

Then the great bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi spoke this vajra samaya mudrā of his own:
hūṃ vajra matha

Then the great bodhisattva Vajragarbha spoke this jewel samaya mudrā of his own:
hūṃ bhṛkuṭī vajra rata

Then the great bodhisattva Vajranetra spoke this Dharma samaya mudrā of his own:
hūṃ mahā padma vajra dṛḍha

Then the great bodhisattva Vajraviśva spoke this karma samaya mudrā of his own:
hūṃ vajra karma agra kṛte

Then again the great bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi explained the maṇḍala of the four mudrās of Victorious over the Three Worlds.

Next, what is to be fully explained
Is the sublime maṇḍala of the mudrās.
Because it resembles the Vajradhātu,
It is called Vajracaṇḍa.
In the manner of the great maṇḍala,
Mark out the whole maṇḍala with cords.
In all directions around the Buddha
Draw Victorious over the Three Worlds and the rest.

Then, in this maṇḍala, having performed the extensive rite of summoning and so on in the manner of the great maṇḍala, and having had the disciple enter, one should learn the wisdom of the secret mudrās of the maṇḍala of the four mudrās.

Having drawn the maṇḍala of the four mudrās oneself,
And recited the purity of the nature of reality,
By dwelling together with a woman,
One will attain accomplishment.
Having drawn the maṇḍala of the four mudrās oneself,
And recited the purity of the nature of reality,
By gazing at a woman with desire,
One will attain accomplishment.
Having drawn the maṇḍala of the four mudrās oneself,
And recited the purity of the nature of reality,
One succeeds by adorning a beautiful woman.
Having drawn the maṇḍala of the four mudrās oneself,
And recited the purity of the nature of reality,
If one embraces her, one attains every accomplishment.

Here the mudrās of the purity of the nature of reality are these: oṃ sarva tathāgata viśuddha dharmate ho oṃ vajra viśuddha dṛṣṭi ja oṃ sarva viśuddha tri hūṃ oṃ sarva viśuddha kāya vaṃ mana karma vajrī hūṃ

Next, one should learn the wisdom of the secret and hidden mudrās of the maṇḍala of the four mudrās.

Having entered this maṇḍala,
And delighted a supreme woman
With the five qualities of sense pleasure,
One will attain the highest accomplishment.

Then its heart-mantra is spoken:
ho vajra kāma

Then, having learned with the extensive rite the four samaya mudrās, beginning with the trident mudrā pressed down by the vajra, in the proper way, and having struck vajra cymbals, drums and percussion in the manner of these four mudrās, one should sing and offer the heart-mantra of the Vajrasattva compendium, and then make offering with the mudrās, the responding mudrās, the subsidiary mudrās, the wisdom mudrās and the offering of dance. Here the offering made with the offering of dance is this:

Through the application of the vajra dance,
The two fingers of Vajracaṇḍa
In the mudrā of Vajra Hūṃ
Should be bound right at the heart.
Likewise, dancing, the left hand
Should be grasped by the right fist,
And turning it, on the forehead
One should place the face of the fingers.
Likewise, releasing the dance,
Join an even lotus bud,
And placing the fingers at the heart,
Bow with a heart that is moved.
Evening the fingers of Vajracaṇḍa,
Bending them, join their tips,
And turning them from there, at the crown of the head
Place the fingertips.

Then again the great bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi spoke this wrathful samaya of his own vajra samaya:
hūṃ

Then its maṇḍala is explained.

Next, what is to be fully explained
Is the sublime secret maṇḍala.
Because it resembles the Vajradhātu,
It is called Vajra Hūṃ.
In the manner of the great maṇḍala
One should draw the whole maṇḍala.
In its center, drawn well,
Is the vajra holder on a moon disk,
With the great mudrā of Vajra Hūṃ,
Holding a vajra in his hand,
Standing in the posture with one leg extended and one drawn in,
With color and form made as they should be.

Then here, having performed the whole extensive rite of the maṇḍala of the secret mudrās, one should learn the wisdom of the secret mudrā called Vajra Hūṃ.

Having entered this maṇḍala,
And having practiced by placing
The finger of Victorious over the Three Worlds in the opening,
One will accomplish all rites.

Then this heart-mantra of practice is spoken:
hūṃ vajra samaya krita

Then one should learn the wisdom of the mudrā called Vajra Hūṃ.

Having duly entered the maṇḍala,
Abiding in the supreme great mudrā,
When one abides in it, it is called Vajra Hūṃ:
One will perform all rites.

Here he spoke its heart-mantra of practice:
hūṃ vajra samya hūṃ

Then, having learned the binding of the four mudrās in the proper way, one will in the same way gain accomplishment. The accomplishments are shared by everything drawn on cloth and the like as in the maṇḍala, and by all images, cast ones and the rest. This is the maṇḍala of the single mudrā.

Then Vajrapāṇi called upon all the tathāgatas and spoke these words: "Blessed Ones, may all the tathāgatas empower this family of mine, so that all sentient beings, even when they act just as they please, may attain all accomplishments."

Then again the Blessed Ones, all the tathāgatas, gathered together and, in order to empower the King of Ritual Manuals of Victorious over the Three Worlds, declared:

"Vajrasattva, excellent are you!
Vajraratna, excellent are you!
Vajradharma, you too are excellent!
Vajrakarma, you are most excellent!
This sūtra has been well taught:
The unsurpassed Vajra Vehicle,
The secret of all the tathāgatas,
The very essence of the Great Vehicle."

Here is completed the extensive rite of the Ritual Manual of the Great Vajra, from the Great Ritual Manual of the Vajra Samaya of All Tathāgatas.

Then again the Blessed Ones, all the tathāgatas, gathered together and, though they knew, spoke these words to the bodhisattva, the great being Vajrapāṇi: "Listen to the command of the Blessed Ones, all the tathāgatas: you too must release this body of Maheśvara (Śiva) from beneath your foot."

Then the Blessed Vajrapāṇi addressed all the tathāgatas with these words: "The Blessed One empowered me as the wrathful one who tames all the wicked, and I have pressed him down. How then am I to release him?"

Then, in order to restore life to the body of Maheśvara, lord of all three worlds without exception, all the tathāgatas drew forth from their own hearts this heart mantra that summons the consciousness of the dead:

oṃ vajrasatva hūṃ ja

Then the binding of its mudrā was explained:

Bind the Secret Hook tightly,
And extend the little fingers together;
When it is placed on the head of the dead,
Life will be regained once more.

Then, the instant it was obtained, the Blessed One, the tathāgata Bhasmeśvaranirghoṣa, came back from the world system called Covered with Ashes, entered that body of Īśvara, and uttered this inspired utterance:

How wonderful! The wisdom of the buddhas
Of all the buddhas is unsurpassed,
Since even this dead body
Has become an element of life.

Then the bodhisattva, the great being Vajrapāṇi, spoke this heart mantra called Lifting the Foot:

oṃ vajra muḥ

Then the binding of its mudrā was explained:

Joining the tips of the vajra-wrath fingers well,
Binding them tightly and placing them,
One turns those vajras around
And draws them out from beneath.

Then, the instant these were spoken, the Great God (Śiva) knew for himself that he was freed from beneath the feet of the great Vajradhara. Then that tathāgata blessed the body of Maheśvara to revive and, for the benefit of all sentient beings in this world system and in order to tame all the wicked, installed it as his own regent.

Then from the sole of Vajrapāṇi's foot there arose the mudrā of the awakening mind of all the tathāgatas, called Moon Foot:

oṃ candrottāre samantabhadrakiraṇi mahāvajriṇi hūṃ

Then the binding of its mudrā was explained:

Binding the vajra bond tightly,
With thumbs and little fingers joined evenly,
When, so joined, they are well extended,
It is known as the Vajra Line.

Then, the instant it arose, the tathāgata Candrottara emerged from that sole of the foot, and on the head of Maheśvara, at the mark where Vajrapāṇi's foot had pressed, a half moon came to rest and remained there on the left side.

Then all the tathāgatas, as a companion to Vajrapāṇi, placed a vajra trident in his hand and consecrated him with the vajra name "Vajravidyottama, Vajravidyottama," conferring the consecration. Then Vajravidyottama, the bodhisattva, the great being, whirling that vajra trident like a wheel of firebrand, made offering with the offering of dance and uttered this inspired utterance:

How wonderful! The awakening mind
Of all the buddhas is unsurpassed:
Touched in the dance by the supreme foot,
One gains accomplishment as buddhahood.

Then the bodhisattva, the great being Vajrapāṇi, rose from that samādhi of the wrathful one and addressed the Blessed One with these words: "Blessed One, all the tathāgatas placed the vajra in my hand and consecrated me as Vajrapāṇi. Therefore I shall arrange the outer vajra family, the gods and the rest, in their stations within this great maṇḍala of Victorious over the Three Worlds, and so make those beings irreversible from unsurpassed, perfect and complete awakening."

Then the Blessed Vairocana, the tathāgata, the arhat, the perfectly and completely awakened buddha, spoke this uṣṇīṣa of all the tathāgatas:

oṃ vajrasatva uṣṇīṣa hūṃ phaṭ

Then, the instant this was spoken, from the uṣṇīṣas of all the tathāgatas that Blessed Vajrapāṇi became rays of light of many colors, illuminated all world systems, and then gathered together again and settled on the crown of the Blessed Vajrapāṇi in the form of the uṣṇīṣa of all the tathāgatas. Then from that mass of splendor there arose this uṣṇīṣa of all the tathāgatas:

oṃ sarvatathāgatoṣṇīṣa anavalokite mūrdhani tejorāśi oṃ hūṃ jvala jvala dhaka dhaka dara dara vidara vidara cchinda cchinda bhinda bhinda hūṃ hūṃ phaḍ phaḍ svāhā

Then the bodhisattva, the great being Vajrapāṇi, spoke this supreme vidyā of his own:

oṃ nisumbha vajra hūṃ phaḍ

Then Vajrapāṇi drew forth this heart mantra from his own heart:

oṃ ṭakki jaḥ

Then the bodhisattva, the great being Vajragarbha, spoke this supreme vidyā of his own:

oṃ vajraratna uttama jñālaya hūṃ phaṭ

Then the great bodhisattva Vajranetra spoke this supreme vidyā of his own:

oṃ svabhāvaśuddhāḥ vajrapadma śodhaya sarvaṃ vidyottama hūṃ phaṭ

Then the great bodhisattva Vajraviśva spoke this supreme vidyā of his own:

oṃ vajrakarma uttama vajradhara samayamanusmara sumbha nisumbha ākarṣaya praveśaya āveśaya bhanadhaya samayaṃ grahaya sarvakarmāṇi mai kuru mahāsatva hūṃ phaṭ

Then the bodhisattva, the great being Vajravidyottama, offered his own heart mantra in order to pay homage at the feet of the Blessed Vajrapāṇi:

oṃ sumbha nisumbha vajravidyottama hūṃ phaṭ

Then the bodhisattva Vajracaṇḍa, king of vidyās, bowed at both feet of the Blessed One and spoke this heart mantra of his own:

oṃ vajraśūla

Then Vajramāyā, king of vidyās, spoke this supreme heart of his own vidyā:

oṃ vajrasamaya dhariṣaya sarvaṃ hūṃ phaṭ

Then Vajraghaṇṭā, king of vidyās, spoke this supreme vidyā of his own:

oṃ vajraghaṇṭa raṇa raṇa hūṃ phaṭ

Then Vajramuni offered his own heart mantra:

oṃ vajramon mahāprajñā hūṃ phaṭ

Then Vajrāyudha offered his own heart mantra:

oṃ vajrāsphudhadāmaka hūṃ phaṭ

These are the kings of vidyās.

Then Vajrakuṇḍalī, the vajra wrathful one, bowed at the feet of the Blessed Vajrapāṇi and offered him this heart mantra of his own:

oṃ vajrakuṇḍali mahāvajrakrodha gṛhṇa hana hana daha daha paca vidhvansaya vajreṇa mudanaṃ balayati bhṛtasahṛdaya vajrakrodha hūṃ phaṭ

Then Vajraprabha, the vajra wrathful one, offered this heart mantra of his own:

oṃ vajraprabha māraya syoma krodha hūṃ phaṭ

Then Vajradaṇḍa, the vajra wrathful one, offered this heart mantra of his own:

oṃ vajradaṇḍaya sarvāduṣṭa mahākrodha hūṃ phaḍ

Then the vajra wrathful one Vajrapiṅgala offered this heart mantra of his own:

oṃ vajrapiṅgala viśaya sarvaduṣṭaṃ ṣḍaṃ vi mahākrodha huṃ phaṭ

These are the vajra wrathful ones.

Then Vajra Elephant Trunk, lord of hosts, offered this heart mantra to the Blessed Vajrapāṇi:

oṃ vajraśodha mahāgaṇapati rakṣa sarvaduṣṭebhyo vajradhara ājñāvaya hūṃ phaṭ

Then Vajramālā offered this heart mantra of his own:

oṃ māla gaṇpati mālaya ākarṣaya praveśaya vaśaṃ kuru māraya hūṃ phaḍ

Then Vajravaśī offered his own heart mantra:

oṃ vajravaśi mahāgaṇapati vaśaṃ kuru hūṃ phaḍ

Then Vijayavajra, lord of hosts, offered his own heart mantra:

oṃ vajravijaya vijaya mahāgaṇapati hūṃ phaḍ

These are the lords of hosts (gaṇapatis).

Then Vajramusala, the vajra messenger, offered this heart mantra of his own to Vajrapāṇi:

oṃ vajramusala kuru sarvaduṣṭaṃ huṃ phaṭ

Then Vajrānila, the messenger, offered his own heart mantra:

oṃ vajranīla mahāvega gaṇapati sarvaduṣṭa hūṃ phaṭ

Then Vajrānala, the messenger, offered his own heart mantra:

oṃ vajra anala mahāduṣṭa jvālaya sarva bhasmi kuru sarvaduṣṭaṃ hūṃ phaḍ

Vajrabhairava, the great messenger, offered his own heart mantra:

oṃ vajrabhairava vajra hūṃ davaśala sarvaduṣṭana mahāyakṣa hūṃ phaṭ

These are the messengers.

Then Vajrāṅkuśa, the vajra servant, offered this heart mantra of his own to the Blessed Vajrapāṇi:

oṃ vajra aṃkuśa ākarṣaya sarvaṃ mahācitta hūṃ phaṭ

Then Vajrakāla offered his own heart mantra:

oṃ vajrakāla mahāmṛtyu mutbāda hūṃ phaṭ

Then Vajravināyaka offered his own heart mantra:

oṃ vajravināyakasya vighnan kuru hūṃ phaṭ

Then Vajra Nāgavajra bowed at the feet of Vajrapāṇi and offered this heart mantra of his own:

oṃ vajranāgavajra ānaya sarvadanadane hiryagarnane mukta aladkāravini sarvopakāraṇani vajradhara samayamanusmara kata bhinda bhinda hara hara prāṇāṃ mahāccheda hūṃ phaṭ

These are the servants.

Then the bodhisattva, the great being Vajrapāṇi, spoke this samaya for summoning the whole vajra family:

oṃ vajra aṃkuśa ākarṣaya hūṃ phaṭ

Then he spoke this samaya for making them enter:

oṃ vajravāśākaḍḍhya hūṃ

Then he spoke the binding of all:

hūṃ vajrasphoṭa vaṃ

Then he spoke the heart mantra of action:

oṃ vajrakarma samaya krita

Then the Blessed Vajrapāṇi explained this great maṇḍala of all the families:

Next I shall fully explain
The supreme great maṇḍala.
Like the wheel of Dharma,
Lay out the whole maṇḍala with the cord.

For that, this is the heart mantra for laying out the cord:

oṃ sarvavajrasūtraṃ ākarṣaya sarvamaṇḍala hūṃ

In the very center of the maṇḍala
Drive in a peg of khadira wood;
Then make the cord twofold,
And with it lay out the lines.

For that, this is the heart mantra of the peg:

oṃ vajra kīlaya kīlaya sarvavighāṃ bandha hūṃ phaṭ

Having four cords,
Lay out a circular maṇḍala with the cord.
Then, going outside it,
Do the same at twice the measure.
In three tiers of it
Lay out the outer maṇḍala with the cord.
In the intermediate directions, like the spokes of a wheel,
Lay out the corner lines with the cord.
This is the rite of laying out the cord.

Then, into what has been laid out with the cord,
Fill in pure colors.
Holding them in the left vajra fist,
Take them up according to the rite.
First draw, and afterward as you please.

For that, this is the heart mantra of the colors:

oṃ vajraraṅga samaya hūṃ

Then the vajra master as well,
Resting in meditative equipoise and seated at the center,
Should open the four vajra doors,
Even with the mind alone.

For that, this is the heart mantra for opening the doors:

oṃ vajra udghaṭa samaya praveśaya śīghaṃ smara vajrasamaya hūṃ phaṭ

Of gold, or else of silver,
Or else drawn on the ground in the middle,
Or upon a square slab,
Set an image of the Buddha.

For that, this is the heart mantra of all the buddhas:

oṃ sarvavid

On all sides of the Buddha
The four great beings are placed.
In front is Vajrapāṇi,
Who is called Victorious over the Three Worlds.

For that, these are the heart mantras of the great beings:

oṃ sumbha nisumbha hūṃ gṛhṇa gṛhṇa hūṃ gṛhṇāpaya gṛhṇapaya hūṃ ānaya ho bhagavāṃ vajra hūṃ phaṭ

oṃ vajrabhṝkuṭṝ krodha ānaya sarvaṃ ratna hīḥ hūṃ phaṭ

oṃ vajradṛṣṭi krodha dṛṣṭa māraya hūṃ phaṭ

oṃ vajraviśva krodha kuru sarva viśvarūpataya siddhaye hūṃ phaṭ

When entering or coming out,
Imagine slipping beneath the cord,
And pronounce the Vajra Force:
With it, step across the line.

For that, this is the heart mantra of Vajra Force:

oṃ vajra vaigākrama hūṃ

Having stepped across with the Vajra Force,
Likewise, in the first maṇḍala,
Above and below, in their proper order,
Draw Vajramāyā and the others.

For that, these are the samaya heart mantras:

oṃ vajracakra hūṃ oṃ vajraghaṇṭa hūṃ oṃ vajradaṇḍakaśa hūṃ oṃ vajra āyudha hūṃ

Having stepped across with the Vajra Force,
Likewise the second maṇḍala:
Vajrakuṇḍalī and the others,
The vajra wrathful ones, should be placed there.

For that, these are their heart mantras:

oṃ prajvalita dīpta vajra hūṃ oṃ vajrasaume hūṃ oṃ vajradaṇḍa hūṃ oṃ vajravikṛha hūṃ

Having stepped across with the Vajra Force,
In the four doors as well,
Vajra Elephant Trunk and the others,
All of them, draw in their order.

These are their samaya heart mantras:

oṃ vajradhama hūṃ oṃ vajramāla hūṃ oṃ vajra artha hūṃ oṃ vajra asi hūṃ

Having stepped across with the Vajra Force,
What is drawn in the third maṇḍala,
Above and below in their proper order,
Is Vajramusala and the others.

These are their heart mantras:

oṃ vajramusala hūṃ oṃ vajrapāta hūṃ oṃ vajrajvāla hūṃ oṃ vajragraha hūṃ

Having stepped across with the Vajra Force,
What is drawn in the fourth maṇḍala
Is Vajrāṅkuśa and the other servants,
Above and below in their order.

These are their heart mantras:

oṃ vajradaṃṣṭrā huṃ oṃ vajramāraṇa hūṃ oṃ vajravega hūṃ oṃ vajraharaṇa hūṃ

Having stepped across with the Vajra Force,
In laying out the outer maṇḍala,
Above and below in their proper order,
Draw all the mātṛkā goddesses.
Then at the four vajra doors as well
Place the doorkeepers likewise.
Then what is further explained
Accords with the extensive rite.

Then, having performed in this great maṇḍala of the wheel of the three worlds all the actions such as summoning, the vajra master himself binds the mudrā of the vajra wrathful one Tirintiri and commands the disciple thus: "I shall generate in you the wisdom of the vajra samaya. Do not speak of it to anyone, lest, by failing to avoid calamity, you meet your death and fall into hell." Having commanded this, he gives this heart mantra of the oath. Also show the mudrā of the vajra wrathful one Tirintiri:

If you transgress the samaya,
The samaya of the great vajra wrathful one
Will burn your family,
And, beginning from the brain,
Will bring ruin upon your bodies.

This is the binding of the samaya.

Then one binds with this heart mantra, the karma mudrā of holding the vajra:

oṃ sarvatathāgata vajradhāra gṛhṇa bandha samaya hūṃ

Then the binding of this mudrā is explained:

Place the two hands one above the other,
And link thumbs and little fingers:
This samaya, the karma mudrā,
Is also called the Vajra Bond.

Then one consecrates with vajra water. The heart mantra is this:

oṃ vajra abhiṣeka abhiṣiñca vajradhāratasamagragraṃ

Then one covers the face with a veil and makes the disciple enter with this heart mantra:

oṃ vajra braveśaya vajra āveśaya adhitiṣṭha vajra hūṃ

Then, once the disciple has entered, a flower is cast with this heart mantra:

oṃ pratīccha adhitiṣṭha vajra hoḥ

Then, by whichever deity the flower falls upon, he will attain accomplishment. Then the face veil is removed and the maṇḍala is shown in its proper sequence, but the deity that is his is not shown to him. Why is that? Because there are sentient beings of wrong view who would not have faith, thinking, "How could this be the unfailing wisdom of the buddhas, the blessed ones? How could one whom Vajrapāṇi consecrated as a tathāgata in the vajra family be a tathāgata at all?" This is the samaya. Otherwise, it should be shown to those who have faith in the deities, after this heart mantra of the oath has been given to them.

Then he consecrates the disciple with the garland of vajra jewel insignia, places the karma vajra in both hands, bestows a vajra name, and does everything just as in the great maṇḍala of the vajra samaya.

Then the binding of the great mudrās is taught:

Binding the vajra bond tightly,
And joining the thumbs inside,
Bend the index fingers and cover them with both:
This is called the Sattva Uṣṇīṣa.

Having made the vajra bond,
Raise thumbs, middle fingers and little fingers:
This is called the Mass of Splendor,
The noble one of great splendor.

Binding the two vajra mudrās,
Linking thumbs and little fingers,
Bind the Hook tightly:
This belongs to the Great Supreme Vidyā.

Having bound the mudrā
Of the Great Supreme Vidyā, bind it firmly
At the heart with the faces of the thumbs:
This is declared the heart of the Sattva.

From that same mudrā, the middle and ring fingers,
These digits firmly bound,
Turned in the manner of Vajraratna,
Should be placed at the face.

Producing that same mudrā and placing it,
Turn it again at the heart:
This is called the Fourfold Flower,
The supreme vidyā of the lotus.

That same, beginning from the crown of the head,
Circle around the maṇḍala of the body:
This mudrā of the crossed vajra
Fully accomplishes Vajrakarma.

Binding the Sattvī Vajra tightly,
And raising the little fingers as a vajra:
This is the heart of all the vidyās;
This mudrā accomplishes everything.

Binding thumb and little finger,
The three middle fingers of the left
Form the three prongs, with the middle one as the tip,
Holding the vajra mudrā:
This supreme vajra vidyā
Is called Vajraśūla.

Then what is further explained
Is the examination of Vajramāyā and the others.
Binding the vajra bond tightly,
One should bind the left vajra:
This is declared the Vajra Fist,
And it belongs to the whole vajra family.
Making that vajra twofold,
With it all the emblems are placed as well.
The mudrās of the entire vajra family
Arise from it as well:
Fingers extended and placed above,
Index finger joined with thumb,
Placing it generally with "oṃ,"
And placing it above the vajra.

These are the assemblies of the great mudrās of the kings of vidyās.

The hand held well extended,
Likewise on the back of the hand,
The fist clenched and placed, the arm fiercely bent,
And turned toward the mouth.

This is the assembly of the mudrās of the vajra wrathful ones.

The left thumb placed above,
Joined in the manner of binding a garland,
The right in the gesture of giving wealth,
And the sword mudrā, the supreme fist.

These are the great mudrās of the lords of hosts, gathered together.

The right hand holding a pestle in front,
Likewise the hand extended,
With the right showing blazing light,
And the vajra fist shaken.

This is the assembly of the great mudrās of the messengers.

Fangs at the tips of the index fingers, with a scowl,
Striking with the staff and holding firm,
Bending the arm and drawing it out,
And the right drawn by the left.

This is the great assembly of the mudrās of the servants.

When they are shown throughout the maṇḍala,
It is done holding the vajra with the left hand.
Then what is further explained
Is accomplishment and action.

Whatever sattva, whatever his mudrā,
If one, oneself and by oneself,
Becomes thoroughly familiar with it as oneself,
The supreme mudrā will be accomplished.

The great uṣṇīṣa is a great protection;
The Mass of Splendor bestows full accomplishment;
The one called Vajra Hūṃ accomplishes everything;
Placed at the heart, it summons all.

These are the mudrās of the Buddha.

The vidyā of all accomplishes all:
It is the Supreme Vajra Vidyā.
Vajraśūla, the great mudrā,
Bestows the great accomplishment.
The vajra of illusion bestows full accomplishment;
With the bell one brings about true descent;
With the wooden staff one passes into nirvāṇa;
The vajra of vajras slays.

These are the kings of vidyās.

The supreme blazing one tames the wicked;
The supreme gentle one slays all;
The supreme staff is slaying;
The terrifying eye strikes fear.

These are the mudrās of the vajra wrathful ones.

The intoxicating one makes utterly intoxicated;
The garland is declared to do all things;
The one who makes desire brings joy;
The slayer slays all.

These are the mudrās of the hosts.

The pestle crushes the vicious;
Likewise the woven cloth;
The supreme blazing one burns the vicious;
The yakṣiṇī calls the grahas.

These are the mudrās of the messengers.

The vajra fang devours;
The slayer slays all;
The obstructress thoroughly obstructs;
And Hārītī carries off all.

These are the mudrās of the servants.

Here is completed the extensive rite of the great maṇḍala of the wheel of the three worlds, from the King of Ritual Manuals of the Great Vajra Samaya of All Tathāgatas.

Then again the Blessed One entered the samādhi called the Blessing Arisen from the Samaya of the Vajra Dhāraṇī of All Tathāgatas, and spoke this supreme vidyā of his own:

oṃ vajravasitre svāhā

Then again the great bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi spoke this supreme vidyā of his own:

oṃ vajradhari hūṃ vajravikrame hūṃ phaṭ

Again the great bodhisattva Vajragarbha spoke this supreme vidyā of his own:

oṃ vajraratnagotre svāhā

Then again the great bodhisattva Vajranetra spoke this supreme vidyā of his own:

oṃ vajrapadmanaitre srāhā

Then again the great bodhisattva Vajraviśva spoke this supreme vidyā of his own:

oṃ vajrakarmakare svāhrā

Then again the great bodhisattva Vajravidyottama spoke this supreme vidyā of his own:

oṃ vajraśūlāgre svāhā

Then Vajramāyā, king of vidyās, spoke this supreme vidyā of his own:

oṃ vajracakre hūṃ

Vajraghaṇṭā said:

oṃ vajraghaṇḍake hūṃ

Vajramuni said:

oṃ vajradantakaṣṭhe hūṃ

Vajrāyudha said:

oṃ vajre hūṃ

These are the samaya mudrās of the kings of vidyās.

Then Vajrakuṇḍalī, the vajra wrathful one, spoke this samaya mudrā of his own heart mantra:

oṃ vajrajvāle hūṃ

Then Vajraprabha said:

oṃ vajrasaume hūṃ

Then Vajradaṇḍa said:

oṃ vajradaṇḍe hūṃ

Vajrapiṅgala said:

oṃ vajraviṣāṇi hūṃ

These are the samaya mudrās of the vajra wrathful ones.

Then Vajra Elephant Trunk spoke his own samaya mudrā:

oṃ vajramede hūṃ

Vajramālā said:

oṃ vajramāle hūṃ

Vajravaśī said:

oṃ vajra abhiṣe hūṃ

Vijayavajra said:

oṃ vajra aparājita hūṃ

These are the samaya mudrās of the lords of hosts.

Then Vajramusala spoke his own samaya mudrās:

oṃ vajramusalagrahe hūṃ

Then Vajrānila said:

oṃ vajravāte hūṃ

Vajrānala said:

oṃ vajrajvāle hūṃ

Vajrabhairava said:

oṃ vajragrahe hūṃ

These are the samaya mudrās of the vajra messengers.

Then Vajrāṅkuśa said:

oṃ vajradaṃṣṭre hūṃ

Vajrakāla said:

oṃ vajravaśe hūṃ

Vajravināyaka said:

oṃ vajravighnan hūṃ

Vajra Nāgavajra said:

oṃ vajraharaṇi hūṃ

These are the samaya mudrās of the vajra servants.

Then again Vajrapāṇi spoke this vajra maṇḍala of the whole vajra family:

Next, what is to be explained
Is the supreme vajra maṇḍala.
For peaceful accomplishment, the door is to the north;
Lay out the outer maṇḍala with the cord.
Within it, there as well,
Likewise make the door first to the east.
In its center, as is fitting,
An image of the Buddha should be placed.
Victorious over the Three Worlds and the others,
Those four, are placed on all its sides.
Likewise, as the principal of the maṇḍala,
The vajra mudrā should be drawn.
In all of its directions
The mudrās of the family should be drawn.
Vajra Elephant Trunk and the others,
Those four, are placed on all its sides.
Likewise, as the principal of the maṇḍala,
The vajra mudrā should be drawn.
In all of its directions
The mudrās of the family should be drawn.
Vajra Elephant Trunk and the others,
The four, are the guardians of the doors.
Bhīmā, Śrī, the Mistress of Sound,
And Durgā, from the left-hand corners:
The mudrās of the outer corners,
These very ones, should be drawn.
In the outer maṇḍalas
The goddesses should be drawn.
Then the extensive rites
Shall be explained just as they are.

Then, in this vajra maṇḍala, having done everything as one wishes and having performed the samaya actions with Vajrāṅkuśa and the others, bind the disciple according to the rite of holding the vajra, and command him: "Do not speak of these samaya mudrās in front of anyone who has not seen the samaya. Do not spread the secret." Then place the karma vajra upon the mudrā of holding the vajra and make him enter according to the rite. Once he has entered, have him let fall that vajra in the same way. Whichever deity it falls upon, he comes under the power of that one's samaya mudrā, and by it he performs all actions. Then remove the face veil, show him the maṇḍala in its order, and tell him the secret of the samaya mudrās:

This samaya mudrā of yours
Will always perform every action:
It is like mothers and sisters,
Like wives and daughters.

For that, these are its heart mantras:

oṃ sarvagamani sarvavāktre svadhaya guhyavajriṇi hūṃ phaṭ

By reciting this once, one brings all women under one's power, and if one has relations with them it will not become contrary to the Dharma. Having enjoyed whatever one pleases, one should practice. Then, knowing all the mudrās with the mind for the purification of all sentient beings, one also performs all the supreme actions. Thus spoke the Blessed Vajradhara.

Then the samaya mudrās are to be learned:

The samaya wrathful one, index fingers raised as a crest,
Should be bound tightly at the heart as well.
From the ūrṇā at the brow, draw it out from the mouth;
Placing it at the crown, make it twofold.
With the index finger of the left vajra
Press down the three-pointed spearhead.
By binding this, perfectly,
The Supreme Vidyā will be accomplished as well.
The whole vajra family
Is gathered in the left vajra as its principal.
In accordance with the samaya rite,
I shall explain the binding of the mudrās.
For all, the wheel, with fingertips drawn together;
The bell mudrā likewise;
Likewise the mudrā of oṃ,
And making the lion's ears.

These are the mudrās of the kings of vidyās.

Holding the blaze all around,
And light, perfectly gathered,
Holding the fist of the staff,
And circling the cheek mudrā.

These are the samayas of the wrathful ones.

The drinking mudrā and the garland,
Pressing the vajra, which is the knot,
And placing at the crown, which belongs to the hosts:
These are the protection of the maṇḍala.

These are the samayas of the hosts.

The hand bent and placed at the mouth,
And circled to the back as well;
The blazing one emitting sparks,
Opened and then held at the mouth.

These are the samayas of the female messengers.

The two last fingers placed in the mouth,
Squeezing and pressing hard,
Crossing by crossing the arms,
And swiftly snatching likewise.

These are the samayas of the female servants.

Here is completed the extensive rite of the vajra dhāraṇī maṇḍala of the whole vajra family, from the Great King of Ritual Manuals of the Vajra Samaya of All Tathāgatas.

Then again the Blessed One entered the samādhi called the Blessing of Vajradhātu Arisen from the Dharma Samaya of All Tathāgatas, and spoke this supreme vidyā of his own:

oṃ vajravid

Then again Vajrapāṇi spoke this Dharma samaya of his own:

oṃ hana hana vajra hūṃ phaṭ

Then again Vajragarbha spoke this Dharma samaya of his own:

oṃ hara hara hūṃ phaṭ

Then again Vajranetra spoke this Dharma samaya of his own:

oṃ mara mara hūṃ phaṭ

Then again Vajraviśva spoke this Dharma samaya of his own:

oṃ kuru kuru hūṃ phaṭ

Then Vajravidyottama spoke this Dharma samaya of his own:

oṃ hūṃ hūṃ phaṭ

Then Vajramāyā said:

oṃ cchinda cchinda hūṃ phaṭ oṃ āviśa āviśa hūṃ phaṭ oṃ bhūrbuvasvaḥ hūṃ phaṭ oṃ bhindha bhindha hūṃ phaṭ

These are of the kings of vidyās.

oṃ dama dama hūṃ phaṭ oṃ māraya māraya hūṃ phaṭ oṃ ghātaya ghātaya hūṃ phaṭ oṃ bhaya bhaya hūṃ phaṭ

These are of the wrathful ones.

oṃ dama dama hūṃ phaṭ oṃ bandha bandha hūṃ phaṭ oṃ vaśāṃ bhava hūṃ phaṭ oṃ jaya jaya hūṃ phaṭ

These are of the hosts.

oṃ bhyo bhyo hūṃ phaṭ oṃ ghu ghu hūṃ phaṭ oṃ jvāla jvāla hūṃ phaṭ oṃ khāda khāda hūṃ phaṭ

These are of the messengers.

oṃ khana khana hūṃ phaṭ oṃ mara mara hūṃ phaṭ oṃ gṛhṇa gṛhṇa hūṃ phaṭ oṃ pibha pibha hūṃ phaṭ

These are of the female servants.

Then again Vajrapāṇi spoke this Dharma samaya maṇḍala of the whole vajra family:

Next, what is to be explained
Is the maṇḍala of the great samaya.
Like the wheel of the three worlds,
All the maṇḍalas should be drawn.
The Buddha, Vajradhara and the rest,
All of them are in union.
In the manner of the Dharma maṇḍala,
Place the emblems properly at the heart.

Then, having performed the actions according to the rite in this Dharma samaya maṇḍala, bind the mudrā of holding the vajra, the binding of the vajra bell, and say this: "Do not speak of it to anyone who has not seen the samaya and has not seen the family of the deity." While commanding him so, have him ring the bell. Having given this command, give him the heart mantra of the oath as well, saying:

Just as this bell is rung,
And just as its sound is,
So you, O karma vajra,
Will surely come to ruin,
If you show no respect to the vajra master,
Are unfriendly to your vajra companions,
Or are cruel to the kind and bring them grief:
If you should do these things.

Then remove the face veil, show him the maṇḍala, and teach him the wisdom of the Dharma samaya mudrās:

The Buddha, Vajradhara and the rest
Are just as in the Dharma maṇḍala.
Contemplating the sameness of all,
The Supreme Vajra Vidyā is born.
These beings are like an illusion;
Likewise suffering is like a bell;
All suffering passes into nirvāṇa;
The vajra destroyer is unsurpassed.

These are the samādhis of the kings of vidyās.

To tame beings, the supreme wrathful one;
By the peaceful one, one surely slays;
Like a staff, one strikes them down;
One terrifies those of wrong view.

These are the samādhis of the wrathful ones.

There is no hero like the intoxicated one;
There is no bond like the garland;
Through desire for women one masters beings;
By mere reliance, others are defeated.

These are the samādhis of the hosts.

It is the supreme of punishments by striking;
Among touches, it is that of the wind;
The splendid one is the supreme eater of offerings;
Among foods, blood is the finest.

These are the samādhis of the messengers.

Having entered as the lord of fangs,
All the lords of death dwell in his abode;
There is no obstructor beyond terror;
There is no taste beyond water.

These are the samādhis of the servants.

Here is completed the extensive rite of the Dharma samaya maṇḍala of the whole vajra family, from the Great King of Ritual Manuals of the Vajra Samaya of All Tathāgatas.

Then again the Blessed One entered the samādhi called the Vajra Blessing Arising from the Karma Samaya of All Tathāgatas, and spoke this supreme vidyā of his own:

oṃ vajrakarma pravartani samaye hūṃ

Then the bodhisattva, the great being Vajrapāṇi, spoke this supreme vidyā of his own:

oṃ vajra vilāse pūjāya hūṃ

Then Vajragarbha spoke this supreme vidyā of his own:

oṃ abhiṣeke abhiṣiñca hūṃ

Then Vajranetra spoke this supreme vidyā of his own:

oṃ vajra gīte gāhi hūṃ

Then the bodhisattva Vajraviśva spoke this karma vidyā of his own:

oṃ vajra nṛtya nṛtya hūṃ

Then Vajravidyottama spoke this karma samaya of his own:

oṃ vajra vadyottamanetra vikurab vikurva hūṃ phaṭ

Then Vajracaṇḍa, the goddess of Vajrānala, uttered this karma samaya of her own:

oṃ vajra krodha vajra agne jvāla triśūlanvina hṛdaya vajriṇi hūṃ phaṭ

Then the goddess Vajrahemā uttered this karma samaya of her own:

oṃ vajra hema cchinda cakreṇa vajriṇi hūṃ phaṭ

Then Vajrakumārī uttered this karma samaya of her own:

oṃ vajra kumāri śīghraṃ āveśaya ghaṇṭāśapdena vajra vāṇipriye vajra samaye manusmarṇa hūṃ phaṭ

Then the great goddess Vajraśāntī uttered this karma samaya of her own:

oṃ vajra śānti japa japa aghamālaya sarvā namarya śānta trihya hūṃ phaṭ

Then the great goddess, the Vajra Fist, uttered this karma samaya of her own:

oṃ vajra muṣṭi hana hana vajra bhindha bhindha viḍaya viḍaya sarva duṣṭa hṛdayāni oṃ sambhani sumbha hūṃ hūṃ phaṭ

These are those of the vidyā queens.

Then the wrathful Vajrāmṛtā uttered this karma samaya of her own:

oṃ vajra amṛte sarva duṣṭa gṛhṇa bandha hana sarva vidhvaṃsaye vināśaya cchinda va vikuru muttani taya vajreṇa yeke citma mamukara vinyāka tanta ma dīpta krodha vajri hūṃ phaṭ

Then Vajrakāntī uttered this karma samaya of her own:

oṃ vajra kanti māraya saume rupe prativata rāgena śīghraṃ sphoṭaya vajra dharatyena mahājyosna kārale śitarasmi vajriṇi hūṃ phaṭ

Then the Supreme Lady of Vajradaṇḍa uttered this karma heart mantra of her own:

oṃ vajra daṇḍāgri ghātaya hūṃ phaṭ

Then the great wrathful goddess Vajramālā uttered this karma samaya of her own:

oṃ vajra māle mekhale khana khana śapavadeni vaśiṃ kuru traṃ daṃṣṭrā māraya viśane hūṃ phaṭ

These are the vidyās of the wrathful ones.

Then Vajravalayā uttered the karma samaya of her own:

oṃ vajra valaye me cchinda sini bhinda vajriṇi māraya umadaya piva piva hūṃ phaṭ

Then Vajrāśanā uttered the karma samaya of her own:

oṃ vajra aśane vakrāya sarva duṣṭa vajra daśane śagatidharaṇi manuśamanase āhareṇa raruti saśudci priya majavasānulevana vilepatagratre anaya sarva dantyanyaya hiraṇya suvarṇa ādini gramaya de varakṣaṇi hūṃ phaṭ

Then Vajravāsanā uttered the karma samaya of her own:

oṃ vajra vāsane ānaya sarva sra annavaṇadinyuvakaraṇani śīghraṃ vaśiṃ kuru dīpa deme prayaccha āviśa sadyaṃ kataya vajra koṣadharaṇī hūṃ phaṭ

Then Vajravaśī uttered the karma samaya of her own:

oṃ vajra vaśi ānaya vaśaṃ kuru sarva vriya sarva puruṣa nadasi kuru kruddaṃ prasedaya sarva bhyaḥ vhare bhyo haraya vicaraya kari vajra pataka dhāraṇī hūṃ phaṭ

These are those of the vajra host.

Then Vajradūtī uttered the karma samaya of her own:

oṃ vajra duṣṭe ānaya sarva maṇḍalaṃ praveśaya āveśaya bandhaya sarva karmaṇa me kuru śīghraṃ śīghraṃ lagu lagu trasaya māraya dabyavarebho dharaya vijayaṃ kari vajra vatakadhraraṇī hūṃ phaṭ

Then Vajra Force uttered the samaya of her own:

oṃ vegha vajriṇa ghughu ghughu śavade māraya vighira vidhaṃsaya vajra vāśadhāraṇī hūṃ phaṭ

Then Vajrajvālā uttered the karma samaya of her own:

oṃ vajra jvāla jvālaya sarva vajra jvālaya jvālaya daha daha vasmiṃ kuru hūṃ phaṭ

Then Vajrabhairava uttered the karma samaya of her own:

oṃ vajra vigate praveka daṃṣṭākarāli karāla viśana vākatre śīghraṃ śīghraṃ āveśaya bhakṣaya rudhiraṃ pipa mahāyakṣiṇi vajra pāśadhāraṇī hūṃ phaṭ

These are those of the vajra messengers.

Then Vajramukhī, the vajra servant, uttered this karma samaya of her own:

oṃ vajra muṣṭi ānaya vajra diṣṭi vayanike vatālani vasini khana khana khāhi khāhi sarva mukhe praveśaya sphoṭaya māra māraṇi sarva duṣṭanāṃ vajriṇi śitasi dhāriṇī hūṃ phaṭ

Then Vajrakālī uttered the karma samaya of her own:

oṃ vajra kāli mahāpretarūpiṇi manuṣamāṃsarudhirapriye ihyehi gṛhṇa gṛhṇa bhakṣaya vajra dākinī vajra śṛṃkhale sarva devagaṇmātribhute hara hara prāṇāṃ amukasya kapālamālālaṃkṛta sarva kaye kiñci rāyasi vajra khaṭvāṃ gaṇhāriṇī pretamanuṣ śarīre śīghraṃ āveśaya praveśaya bandhaya vaśi kuru māraya vajra rakṣasi hūṃ hūṃ hūṃ hūṃ phaṭ

Then Vajra Tresses uttered this karma samaya of her own:

oṃ vajra pūtame manuṣaya mansa sarva rudhira mutra purīṣa śale smrā śīghraṃ nareka togarbta hirṇyayā hī śrīghraṃ idam mukasya kuru vajra śodhani kadhāraṇī sarva karmaṇi me kuru svāhā hūṃ

Then Vajramakarā uttered the karma samaya of her own:

oṃ vajra makare grasa grasa śīghraṃ praveśaya pātālaṃ bhakṣaya vajra makaradhāraṇī hūṃ phaṭ

These are those of the vajra servants.

Then Vajrapāṇi again spoke this karma maṇḍala of the whole vajra family.

Next comes the full explanation:

The supreme karma maṇḍala:
In the manner of the vajra maṇḍala,
Lay out all the maṇḍalas with cord.
Lay out all the maṇḍalas with cord.
In all the supreme maṇḍalas
The Buddha should be set, seated in the center.
In their directions, in due order,
The great sattvas are placed.

In its center, together with his consort,
Is Vajravidyottama himself.
The secret ones, Vajralāsyā and the rest,
Worship with dance offerings and the like.
There, following the sequence of the goddesses,
In the manner of the wheel maṇḍala,
Their own mudrās, resembling the mudrās,
Should be drawn performing whatever they do.
For the worship of the buddhas and the vajra holders,
It is done in the manner of the vajra dance.
In the corners and in the quarters of the gates,
Incense and the rest, in due order.

Then, having performed in this karma maṇḍala the rites of summoning and the rest, bind the samaya mudrā of the karma of the vajra holder exactly as it should be, and say this: "You must not speak of this secret rite to anyone at all who has not seen the order of the deities and does not know the rites, lest your samaya be broken." Say that. The vajra master binds the samaya mudrā of the holder of his own karma vajra, looks with a wrathful gaze, and says this:

oṃ vajra tvarya āveśaya praveśaya nṛte vaya sarva karma siddhi mi prayaccha hūṃ āḥ hūṃ a la la la la vajri

Then again, when it has descended upon oneself, one enters by oneself, and one should worship with the dance offerings of the mudrās that resemble the mudrās. From then on, with body, speech, gaze, mind, and the vajra mudrās, one performs all rites as one wishes.

Then remove the covering of the face, and teach the wisdom of the mudrās of the dance offering:

Of the Buddha, the vajra holder, and the rest,
Making the supreme samaya twofold,
To Vajravidyottama
Offer Vajralāsyā and the rest.
All these vidyās as well,
In order, upper and lower, as is fitting,
With the mudrās of the dance offering,
Should worship the karma maṇḍala.
By way of the vajra dance
The great mudrās are summarized.
Having made the supreme samaya twofold,
One should release the karma mudrā.
If with this dance offering
One worships all the lords,
The rites of the great Vajradhara and the rest
Will surely be accomplished.

Here is completed the extensive rite of the maṇḍala of the whole vajra family of all tathāgatas.

Then the Blessed One Vajrapāṇi, having appointed all those of his family for the sake of all sentient beings and established them on the ground of irreversibility, though he knew, addressed the Blessed One thus: "Blessed One, if all the tathāgatas empower me to hold all secrets, what is that secret of the tathāgatas? Please tell me."

Then the Blessed One, all the tathāgatas, entered into the samādhi called the Secret Vajra and spoke this secret of all the tathāgatas:

In whatever way the nature of sentient beings
Leads them to be tamed,
In just that way, for the sake of beings,
Act through attachment and the rest, made pure.

Then Vajrapāṇi spoke this vajra secret of his own:

For the benefit of all sentient beings,
And for the sake of the buddhas' teaching,
Even if one kills all sentient beings,
One will not be stained by wrongdoing.

Then the bodhisattva Vajragarbha spoke this jewel secret of his own:

For the benefit of all sentient beings,
Through application for the buddhas' purpose,
Even if one steals the wealth of others,
One will not be stained by wrongdoing.

Then the bodhisattva Vajranetra spoke this lotus secret of his own:

There is no bliss like that of passion.
Offering that to the Victors,
By resorting to the wives of others for the sake of beings,
One will gain merit.

Then the bodhisattva Vajraviśva spoke this karma secret of his own:

For the benefit of all sentient beings,
And for the sake of the buddhas' teaching,
By performing every kind of action,
One gains exceedingly great merit.

Then the Blessed One, the Tathāgata Vairocana, praised the Blessed One Vajradhara, the holder of secrets, saying "Excellent!":

Excellent are you, Vajrasattva!
Vajraratna, you are excellent!
Vajradharma, you are excellent!
Vajrakarma, you are excellent!
This sūtra has been well spoken:
The unsurpassed Vajra Vehicle,
The secret of all the tathāgatas,
The manifest gathering of the Great Vehicle.

Here is completed the Great King of Ritual Manuals called the Vajra Samaya of All Tathāgatas, from the Compendium of the Reality of All Tathāgatas.

The sixth fascicle.

Then again all the tathāgatas gathered together as one, and the Blessed One Vajrapāṇi himself entreated Avalokiteśvara, the lord of all dharmas, with these hundred and eight names:

Lotus sattva, great lotus,
Lord of the world, great lord,
Avalokiteśvara, supreme in the teaching,
Vajradharma, homage to you!
King of Dharma, great purity,
King of mind, great intelligence,
Lotus-natured one, great lotus,
Lotus protector, homage to you!
Lotus-born, light of the fine lotus,
Utter purifier in lotus purity,
Vajra lotus, body of the supreme lotus,
Lotus of lotuses, homage to you!
Great manifold one, great light,
Great dread, equal to the great,
Great steadfast one, great hero,
Great hero, homage to you!
Intent of beings, great vehicle,
Great yogin, great forefather,
Enjoyer of bliss, maker of bliss, aim of purity,
Buddha lotus, homage to you!
Dharma that is suchness, ultimate Dharma,
Pure Dharma, upholder of the Dharma,
Great Dharma and supreme good Dharma,
Wheel of Dharma, homage to you!
Buddha sattva, supreme good mind,
Dharma sattva, holder of the supreme Dharma,
Holy sattva, holder of the supreme mind,
Sattva of sattvas, homage to you!
Avalokiteśvara, supreme protector,
Great protector, the all-seeing one,
Light of the world, aim of the world,
Protector of the world, homage to you!
Syllable of the world, great syllable,
Supreme syllable, likeness of syllables,
Syllable of syllables, seer of all,
Syllable of the wheel, homage to you!
Padmapāṇi, great hand,
Giver of solace, the giver,
Dharma of the buddhas, great buddha,
Buddha-natured one, homage to you!
Form of the buddhas, great form,
Vajra form, bearer of excellent form,
Light of the Dharma, supreme splendor,
Light of the world, homage to you!
Lotus glory, supreme protector,
Dharma glory, protector, possessed of protection,
Protector of Brahmā, great Brahmā,
Son of Brahmā, homage to you!
Supreme light of lights, chief of lights,
Clear light, utterly luminous one,

Protector of light, great light,
Light of the buddhas, homage to you!
Empowerment of the buddhas, supreme buddha,
Son of the buddhas, great learned one,
Empowerment of the buddhas, supreme crown,
Buddha of buddhas, homage to you!
Eye of the buddhas, great eye,
Of the great seer with the Dharma eye,
Possessor of every samādhi and wisdom,
Vajra eye, homage to you!
These qualities that embody all,
Your hundred and eight names:
When one praises with them and grows familiar with them,
One will attain the state of the Lord of the World.
Hero, we entreat you:
Having brought forth your own family,
The holy wheel of Dharma,
We ask you, great Sage, to teach it.

Then the bodhisattva, the great being Avalokiteśvara, having heard the words of entreaty of all the tathāgatas, sat facing toward where the Blessed One, the Tathāgata Śākyamuni, was, placed that vajra lotus at his heart, and uttered this inspired utterance:

How wonderful!
This utterly pure vajra lotus of mine:
It is the father, and this is my son.
Please bless this family!

Then the Blessed One, the Tathāgata Vairocana, entered into the samādhi called the Lotus Blessing Arising from the Vajra Dharma Samaya of All Tathāgatas, and drew forth from his own heart this heart of all the tathāgatas, called the Dharma Samaya of All Tathāgatas:

hrīḥ

The very moment it emerged, from the hearts of all the tathāgatas there emerged lotus-like rays of light, of many kinds of colors, forms, signs, modes of conduct, and radiance. In all world systems they purified attachment and the rest and thoroughly refined the wisdom of dharmatā, and then they turned back again and entered the heart of the noble Avalokiteśvara.

Then the Blessed One, by means of what is called the Dharma Samaya of All Tathāgatas, spoke this supreme vidyā of his own:

oṃ vajra padmodtama hrīḥ

Then the bodhisattva, the great being Vajrapāṇi, spoke this supreme vidyā of his own:

oṃ vajra vidyā hūṃ phaṭ

Then the bodhisattva, the great being Vajragarbha, spoke this supreme vidyā of his own:

oṃ vajra ratnottama tī

Then the bodhisattva, the great being Vajranetra, spoke this supreme vidyā of his own:

oṃ vajra vidyottama hrīḥ

Then the bodhisattva, the great being Vajraviśva, spoke this supreme vidyā of his own:

oṃ vajra vidyottama aḥ

Then the bodhisattva, the great being, the noble Avalokiteśvara, entered into the samādhi called Displaying All Forms, and spoke this heart mantra of his own, called the Samaya of Taming All Beings:

oṃ vajra hūṃ hrīḥ ho

The very moment it was uttered, from the heart of the noble Avalokiteśvara that very Blessed One Vajradhara, holding the form of the noble Avalokiteśvara, entered the lotus. Holding the forms of all the sattvas, the tathāgatas and the rest, with the color, form, attire, and manifold ornaments of one who holds a lotus as the emblem of his mudrā, he took the body of a great bodhisattva and emerged. In all world systems he displayed individual forms according to how each sentient being is to be tamed, and tamed the realm of sentient beings entirely, leaving none out. Then he turned back again and, in the manner of the great Vajradhātu maṇḍala, took his seat upon the moon disks in all the directions of the Blessed One, the Tathāgata Śākyamuni, and uttered this inspired utterance:

How wonderful! The skillful means of all the buddhas
Is compassion itself.
Wherever they tame by skillful means,
They become even gods.

Then again the Blessed One, the bodhisattva, the great being, the noble Avalokiteśvara, brought forth his own family and offered it to all the tathāgatas, so that the cause might be found for the result of giving fearlessness to all sentient beings, of benefit, of supreme attainment, of the wisdom of the vajra dharmatā, and of the superknowledges; and he spoke this great maṇḍala called Taming All Beings.

Next comes the full explanation:

The great maṇḍala, the supreme,
Because it resembles the Vajradhātu,
Is called Taming Beings.
It is four-sided, with four gates,
Adorned with four archways,
And endowed with four cords,
Adorned with silk banners and garlands.
At all the corners of the maṇḍala,
And at the junctions of the gates and their enclosures,
Adorned with vajra jewels,
Lay out the outer maṇḍala with cord.
Within it, the cord
Should be drawn as a square.
The second, its corners opening into gates,
Is to be made like a lotus.
In the manner of eight pillars,
Draw a lotus of eight petals.
In the center of its anthers
Place an image of the Buddha.

For that, the heart mantra for placing the Buddha is this:

buddha hūṃ

The drawing of the buddhas in all directions:
Abiding in the centers of lotuses,
The vajra, the jewel, the lotus,
And the manifold lotus likewise.

For that, their heart mantras are these:

oṃ hūṃ hrīḥ kṛ

In the maṇḍala of Taming Beings,
Having stepped across with Vajra Force,
There draw Avalokiteśvara,
Emanating every form.
In all the directions around him,
In the manner of the vajra swagger and the rest,
Draw the great sattvas, the Buddha and the rest,
Holding lotuses as their emblems.

Their heart mantras are these:

āḥ oṃ tathāgata dharma hūṃ oṃ vajra padma aṃkuśa koṣa dhāra vajra satva hūṃ phaṭ oṃ māraya māraya padma kusuma āyudha dhara amogha śara ho oṃ padma sambha padma hasta sādhu hūṃ

Having stepped across with Vajra Force,
The second maṇḍala is likewise.
What is drawn in its center:
In the midst of a topknot, the Tathāgata too.
In all the directions around it,
In the manner of Bhṛkuṭī and the rest,
Draw them holding lotuses as emblems,
Arranged upper and lower in due order.

For that, their heart mantras are these:

hūṃ oṃ padma bhṛkuṭī ṭi ṭi tra oṃ padma sūrya jrāla hūṃ oṃ padma maṇi ketu dhara candra prahadala avalokiteśvara dehi me sarva artana śrīghraṃ samaya hūṃ oṃ padma aṭṭahāsa eka darśa muka ha ha ha ha hūṃ

Having stepped across with Vajra Force,
The third maṇḍala too is likewise.
The great sattva, in union,
Should be drawn seated upon a lotus.
In all the directions around him,
Arranged upper and lower in due order,
In the manner of the Lotus and the rest,
Place the great sattvas too.

For that, their heart mantras are these:

hrīḥ oṃ tārā padma avalokaya māma samayasatā hūṃ oṃ padma kumāra padma śakti dhara khaḍgena cchinda cchinda hūṃ phaṭ oṃ padma nīla kaṇtha śarak cakra gata padma pāṇi vyāghra carmma nivasani kṛṣṇa sarva kṛta yajño vavāti ajina rmma pa ca ca ntot dhari ya nārāyaṇa rūpa dhare tṛni mukha aṭṭā ṭṭa hāsana praveśaya samayana dehi me siddhi avalokiteśvara hūṃ oṃ brahma padma saṃbhava japa java padma pta śa hūṃ

Having stepped across with Vajra Force,
The fourth maṇḍala too is likewise.
There draw the four-faced Lotus One,
Holding a lotus spear.
In all the directions around him,
In the manner of Vajranṛtyā and the rest,
Draw those holding lotuses as emblems,
Following the rite of the great sattvas.

Their heart mantras are these:

hrīḥ oṃ padma narteśvara nātha pūjaya sarva tathāgata vajra karma samaya ākarṣaya praveśaya bandhaya āveśaya sarva karma siddhi me prayaccha avalokiteśvara hūṃ oṃ vayana dhatada avalokiteśvara rakṣa bandhaya kavace samaye hūṃ oṃ mahā pracaṇṭe viśva rūpa cika padma daṃṣṭa ākarāla viśvan vajra hṛdaya sarva padma yakṣa khāda khāda dhi kdhi kdhi kdhi ke oṃ padma muṣṭi samaya stvaṃ bandha hūṃ phaṭ

Having stepped across with Vajra Force,
What is drawn at all the corners:
In the manner of Vajralāsyā and the rest,
The goddesses Padmalāsyā and the rest.

Their mudrās are these:

oṃ padma lāsye rāgaya mahā devi rāga mahā pūja samya hūṃ oṃ padma mśale abhiṣiñca abhiṣeka pūjā samaya hūṃ oṃ padma gīte gīte pūjā samaya hūṃ oṃ padma nretye nretye sarva pūja pravartan samaye hūṃ

Having stepped across with Vajra Force,
At the corners of the outer maṇḍala,
The four, the Lotus Offering and the rest,
Draw the offering goddesses.

For that, their offering mudrās are these:

oṃ padma ddūpe pūja samaye prahādaya padma kula deyite mahā gaṇi padma rati hūṃ oṃ padma puṣpe pūjā samaya padma vāsini mahā śriye padma kula pratihara sarva artha sādhaya hūṃ oṃ padma dīpa pūjā samaye padma kula sunri mahā dutya āloka vajra vacinaya padma sarasvati hūṃ oṃ padma gandha pūjā samaye mahā padma kula cite kuru sarva karmāṇi me padma siddhi hūṃ

Then at the four lotus gates,
With the whole assembly and the rest, as is fitting,
Draw them well, exactly as they should be,
And draw their heart mantras according to their meaning.

oṃ hayagrīva mahā padma aṃkuśaya ākarṣaya śīghā sarva padma kula samayaṃ padma aṃkuśa dhāra hūṃ jaḥ oṃ amogha pāśa krodha ākarṣaya praveśaya mahā paśupati yama varuṇa kuvera brahma veśa dhāra padma kula samayāṃ hūṃ hūṃ oṃ padma sphoṭa sphoṭa bandha sarva padma kula samayāṃ śīghraṃ hūṃ vaṃ oṃ ṣaṃmukha sanadkumāra veśa dhara padma ghaṇṭe āveśaya sarva padma kula samayāṃ sarva mudrāṃ bandhaya sarva siddhi mme prayaccha padma āpeśa aḥ aḥ aḥ aḥ

Next, the extensive rite of the lotus maṇḍala called Taming All Beings is explained. First of all, the lotus master himself binds the vajra lotus samaya mudrā and enters as is proper; then, in the manner of the great Vajradhātu maṇḍala, he should perform the rites with this heart mantra:

oṃ mahā padma sphoṭa adhitiṣṭha a

Then, having likewise received the command, he empowers himself with the samaya mudrā, learns the lotus emblem, pronounces his own lotus name, and should perform the rites with the Lotus Hook and the rest. Then, with those same Dharma mudrās, he should accomplish the great sattvas. The accomplishments, too, are then obtained in the same way.

Then the lotus disciples should be brought in. First of all, give the lotus disciples the heart mantra of the oath and instruct them thus:

Today, within your heart
The Lotus Sattva himself abides.
Should you speak of this way,
He will at once split it open and depart.

Then instruct them further thus: "Do not speak of this extensive secret rite to anyone at all, lest, not having escaped misery, you die and fall into the hells." Then one should bind the samaya mudrā with this heart mantra:

oṃ vajra padma samaya stvaṃ

Then, having him put on white clothing and covered his face with white silk, he should be brought in. The heart mantra is this:

oṃ padma samaya hūṃ

Then, having performed the rites as is proper, place the lotus emblem in his hand:

oṃ padma hasta vajra dharma hana palaya heni

He in turn should ask, "What is the dharmatā of the lotus?" To him one should reply thus:

Just as this red lotus
Is not stained by the faults of desire,
So, if one meditates on all as pure,
One will not be stained by wrongdoing.

This is the dharmatā in this case.

Then one should teach the wisdom of the mudrās of the lotus family:

Having drawn a lotus at the heart,
If one meditates on the lotus of the heart,
Even the Glory of the Lotus comes under one's power,
So what need is there to speak of ordinary women?
Having drawn the form of a buddha on the forehead,
If one constantly meditates on it,
Through that meditation, swiftly,
One will obtain empowerment.
Within the mouth, upon the tongue,
If one meditates well on the form of a buddha,
The goddess of speech herself
Will always dwell in one's mouth.
At the center of the crown protuberance, a lotus,
Placed there in union:
If one meditates on the crown lotus,
Even flight through the sky comes under one's power.

Their heart mantras are these:

oṃ padma śreyaṃ bhāṣaya ho oṃ padma abhiṣeka prayaccha vaṃ padma sarasthi viśodhaya hūṃ oṃ padma udhagraṃ vaśiṃ kuru jaḥ

Whether on a wall or in the sky,
If one meditates on the holy lotus,
This is the supreme means
Of bringing all sentient beings under one's power.

In the sky or else in another quarter,
Having meditated on the holy lotus,
Whenever one sees it, take it up then,
And one becomes invisible at will.
On a wall or else in the sky,
Having drawn a crossed lotus,
Whenever one has seen it and taken it up,
Then one takes on manifold forms.
In the sky or else in another quarter,
Having meditated on the vajra lotus,
By taking it up, in a single instant
One becomes a lotus vidyādhara.

Their heart mantras are these:

oṃ vajra jagad vaśi jñāna padma āveśaya oṃ jñāma padma tiṣṭha aśana va kuru vaṃ oṃ samādhi viśva padma tiṣṭha rūpaṃ nedharaśaya bhagavāṃ vaṃ oṃ samādhi vajra padmoṣṭha adhitiṣṭha śīghraṃ hri

In all maṇḍalas and the like,
Having drawn Avalokiteśvara,
Before him, with the supreme mudrā
Of Hayagrīva, one should draw beings in.
In all maṇḍalas and the like,
One should draw Avalokiteśvara;
With the mudrā of the unfailing noose
He brings all beings under control.
In all maṇḍalas and the like,
One should draw Avalokiteśvara;
Before him, of the lotus,
Bind the mudrā of the supreme chain.
In all maṇḍalas and the like,
Draw Avalokiteśvara; before him,
By means of the lotus bell,
Is the supreme causing all to be possessed.

For that, their heart mantras are these:

oṃ padma ākarṣaya sarva mahā satvaṃ hūṃ jaḥ oṃ amogha pāśa krodha hūṃ ho hi oṃ padma sphoṭa vaṃ oṃ padma ghanṭa āveśaya sarva aḥ

By oneself, oneself
As the four-faced Lotus sattva
Meditating, and then accomplished,
In an instant one takes on that form.
Thus, oneself by oneself
Abiding in the samādhi of Vajradharma,
If one meditates on the lotus of lotuses,
One attains the inexhaustible state.
At the center of Avalokiteśvara's crown,
If one meditates on oneself, by oneself,
In the form of a buddha,
One becomes equal even to Amitāyus.
Abiding in samādhi through manifold forms,
If in the samādhi of manifold forms
One meditates on oneself,
One becomes equal to Avalokiteśvara.

For that, their heart mantras are these:

padma viśva dharma kaya padma buddha abhiṣeka avalokiteśvara

Next, the wisdom of the mudrās of all the great maṇḍalas is taught. First of all, there is the wisdom of the great mudrā:

Just as in the manner of a painting,
Abiding at the center of a moon disk,
As the sattva abiding upon a lotus,
Meditate on oneself by oneself.

Then their actions are these:

If one binds the great buddha mudrā,
One becomes equal to Amitāyus.
If one binds the vajra lotus,
One becomes equal to Avalokiteśvara.
If one binds with the buddha empowerment,
The Sugata empowers that one.
Through the Lotus one gains the lotus samādhi.
Through the Manifold, the supreme manifold:
One tames by manifold forms
And is empowered with the buddha jewel.
Through the samādhi of the Lotus Sattva,
The Lotus Wrathful One, lordly glory.
Through Vajra Lokeśvara,
The supreme accomplishment of the lotus of desire.
Through the buddha empowerment, buddhahood itself;
The Vajra Lotus grants the supreme attainment.
The Lotus of Desire is endowed with great desire;
Through Joy, excellence is granted.
Through Bhṛkuṭī, wrath is pacified.
The Lotus Sun, supreme splendor;
The Lotus Moon, great beauty;
Through Laughter one laughs aloud and gains attainment;
Through the Liberated One the supreme accomplishment is gained.
Through the Lotus Sword, enjoyments are gained;
Through Nīlakaṇṭha, great summoning;
Through Pāṇḍaravāsinī, accomplishment is gained;
Through the Lotus Lord of Dance it is achieved;
Through the Fearless One, ever free of fear;
Through Pracaṇḍa all, without exception, are tamed;
The Lotus Fist fully accomplishes.
Lāsyā delights, and the Garland is wealth;
Song is sung, Dance gives bliss, Incense satisfies;
Flower is beautiful, and Lamp gives sight;
Through Scent one becomes a supreme fragrance too.
Through Hayagrīva, perfect summoning;
Without any doubt one brings under control.
The Lotus Chain is great at binding;
The Bell causes all to be possessed.

Next is the wisdom of the samaya mudrās of the lotus family:

Having bound the vajra binding,
Raise the thumbs and little fingers evenly.
This mudrā is the Dharma samaya:
It makes one fully attain the Dharma of the buddhas.
Having bound the vajra binding,
Index, middle, and ring fingers even:
That mudrā of the supreme buddha family
Brings about buddhahood itself.
Having bound the vajra binding,
The middle fingers fully even as a vajra:
This is the mudrā of the holy vajra family,
And the vajra is accomplished.
From that, the middle fingers shaped like a jewel
Fully accomplish the vajra jewel.
The middle fingers made in the manner of a lotus
Bring the attainment of the lotus accomplishment.
Having joined the vajra palms,
One comes to perform vajra action.
If one binds the Dharma vajra,
In an instant the samaya is accomplished.
From the vajra binding, by making the index fingers even,
One attains the buddha accomplishment.

Next comes the full explanation:

The sattva mudrās in particular:
Having joined the vajra palms,
And joined the middle fingers evenly,
The thumbs and little fingers spread apart:
This is declared the manifold lotus.
From that, crossing the two thumbs,
Join the tips of the two index fingers and bend them;
The central vajra, the crown topknot:
The topknot is declared to be the Buddha.
Firmly making the vajra binding,
The thumbs even and bowed,
Join the tips of the two index fingers and bend them;
Pressing down, set it into samādhi.
Joining the palms evenly as a lotus,
If the index fingers are even as a vajra,
With the thumbs joined at the sides,
This is the mudrā of the Lord of Certainty.
Having bound the vajra binding,
Pressing well and binding,
Raise the two thumbs as a lotus:
This is declared the Buddha Lotus.
The two thumbs even as a vajra;
Make the index fingers a hook and a sword;
Separate the two little fingers each apart;
Make the middle and ring fingers a lotus.
Joining the lotus fully and evenly,
Grasping index finger with index finger,
And drawing: this is the lotus arrow.
Joining the palms evenly and pressing,
If one binds the mudrā of excellence,
It grants the words "Excellent!":
It is known as the Lotus Excellence.
Joining the palms fully and evenly,
The tips of the index fingers even and bent,
The little fingers spread and placed at the ūrṇā hair:
This is called the Lotus Bhṛkuṭī.
Firmly making the vajra binding,
All the fingers in a circle,
Stretched out fully at the heart:
This is declared the Lotus Sun.
Firmly joining the palms,
Make the two index fingers like a jewel,
Fully adorning the tops of the shoulders:
The Lotus Jewel, the tip of the banner.

Placing the vajra binding at the crown of the head,
Stretching it out fully together with the mouth,
Laughing from one's own mouth,
The faces become eleven.
Binding the lotus of meditative absorption,
The two thumbs are held apart.
From the mudrā of Lotus Tārā,
Bend the two index fingers in the manner of a lotus.
This mudrā of Lotus Tārā
Brings about every accomplishment.
This, made in the manner of a sword,
Is the mudrā of the Lotus Sword.
Make the little fingers a great lotus,
Likewise raise the two thumbs like a staff,
Make the tips of the index fingers a great conch,
Make the middle and ring fingers a wheel.
Making the vajra palms firm,
Insert the right hand inward as oṃ,
And with the left thumb and index finger perform the recitation,
All the gaps like a water-born lotus opening.
Joining the vajra palms evenly,
Likewise from the left and from the right,
Circling them while dancing with grace,
And placing them at the crown: this is she of the lotus.
Making the vajra palms firm,
All the fingertips opened,
Circling, and placing them
At the lotus heart: this is the one who shows.
Making the vajra palms firm,
In the manner of the secret yakṣa,
Stretching out the palms and then drawing them in,
And placing them at the mouth: the Lotus Yakṣiṇī.
Binding the Vajra Fist,
Bend the two middle fingers outward,
And press their backs with one's own thumbs:
This is also called the Lotus Fist.
In the manner of the Vajradhātu,
Arising from the vajra palms,
Are bound the supreme samayas
Of all the offering goddesses.
Making the vajra binding firm,
Joining the two index fingers evenly,
Bending them and joining their tips:
This is declared to be Hayagrīva's.
Joining the lotus palms evenly,
When the two index fingers are knotted in a knot,
That is surely the mudrā of the noose.
Make the thumb and index finger like a chain.
In the manner of the vajra descent,
Joining the lotus palms evenly,
Between the ring and little fingers
Insert the two thumbs and squeeze them tight.

Next, this is the wisdom of the Dharma mudrās of the Lotus family: hrīḥ gṛ kṛ hi śṛ śi dhi hi gṛ ti kṛ bi khi miṣṭi a

Making the two lotus fists,
One should accomplish the karma mudrās.

This completes the extensive rite of the great maṇḍala called Taming All Beings, from the Great King of Ritual Manuals of the Dharma Samaya of All Tathāgatas.

Then again the Blessed One entered into the meditative absorption called "The Blessing Arising from the Samaya of the Dharma Dhāraṇī of All Tathāgatas," and uttered this, his own Supreme Vidyā: oṃ sarba tathāgata dharma samaya hūṃ

Then the great bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi uttered this Supreme Vidyā arising from his own family: oṃ bajra samaye hūṃ

Then the great bodhisattva Vajragarbha uttered this, his own Supreme Vidyā: oṃ maṇi ratna samaye hūṃ

Then the great bodhisattva Vajranetra uttered this, his own Supreme Vidyā: oṃ padma samye hūṃ

Then the great bodhisattva Vajraviśva uttered this, his own Supreme Vidyā: oṃ karma samaye hūṃ

Then the Blessed One, the great bodhisattva Avalokiteśvara, uttered this mudrā maṇḍala of his own family:

Next I shall fully explain
The excellent mudrā maṇḍala,
Which resembles the Vajradhātu
And is called the Lotus Secret.
In the manner of the great maṇḍala,
Lay out the whole maṇḍala with the cord.
In its center, upon a lotus,
Draw the Sovereign Lady of the Vajradhātu.
In all of its directions,
The supreme samayas, as before,
Each endowed with its own vidyā:
Draw the Dharma Vajra and the rest.

Their heart mantras are these: oṃ sarba tathāgata dharmeśvari hūṃ oṃ dharma samaye bajra padmani hūṃ oṃ buddha abhiṣeka ratna samaye hūṃ oṃ tārā samaye hūṃ oṃ biśva mukhe hūṃ

In the maṇḍala of supreme various forms,
